Complete EFNS_DEV Data Dictionary

### How to Read the Complete Dictionary

The inventory remains 440 table fields plus 80 view outputs. Each object has three complementary narrow tables keyed by the exact field name: (A) storage, (B) meaning/use/example, and (C) source, checks and evidence. These are not three different fields. An empty deployed default is written **No default declared**; it does not mean DEFAULT NULL and does not change an explicit bound SQL NULL.

Shared rules: NULL is absence/unknown rather than numeric zero or the text None. True zero and historical signed measures retain their values. Standard-table PK/FK/UNIQUE are declarations, not engine enforcement; NOT NULL and the actual CHECK remain separate. A nullable FK does not guarantee row retention: inspect the actual JOIN and filter. Specific exceptions/validators are listed in C.

All examples are fabricated storage representations, not real records, approved codes, confirmed units or executed submissions. Example parent identities must refer to real example parents to form a valid related dataset. Date/period labels illustrate the converter; they do not establish an EFC-period reporting rule. Signed quota/disposed examples explicitly illustrate history, not daily entry.

Evidence is claim-specific. F001..F520 resolve in Field_Evidence_Index.md and metadata/field_claim_evidence.json: M = deployed storage, P = source mapping, C = local assignment/writer/validator, Q = saved executed counts/results, B = cited business confirmation. An absent category is not inferred. The same type of evidence can support many fields, but not unrelated business assertions. Saved query dates remain 13 October; this revision did not execute new queries.

Units, code meanings, input provenance and business interpretation have separate statuses; technical support is not automatically an approved formula. Current writer capability is not proof that a particular historical row came from it. The explicit gap register identifies unimplemented/unlocated assignment paths. An unlisted field-specific code/check means none was established beyond the shown metadata and table rules, not that validation is disabled. Non-null/zero figures are the saved snapshot counts, not new query results. Full claim details and equivalent expanded notes remain in the JSON/CSV and field evidence index.

APP.AUDIT_EVENT

AUDIT_EVENT records who performed an application operation, its action and entity reference, time and optional sanitized details. One audit event; ENTITY_ID is a polymorphic text reference, not a declared business FK.

A. Storage metadata

FieldSQL typeNullableDefault
AUDIT_IDTEXT(36)NONo default declared
ACTOR_USER_IDTEXT(36)YESNo default declared
ACTOR_EMAILTEXT(320)NONo default declared
ACTIONTEXT(100)NONo default declared
ENTITY_TYPETEXT(100)NONo default declared
ENTITY_IDTEXT(1000)YESNo default declared
DETAILSVARIANTYESNo default declared
OCCURRED_ATTIMESTAMP_TZ(9)NOCONVERT_TIMEZONE('UTC', CAST(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() AS TIMESTAMP_TZ(9)))

B. Definitions, use and examples

FieldDefinition and useIllustrative value
AUDIT_IDWriter-generated persistent record identity for one audit event; ENTITY_ID is a polymorphic text reference, not a declared business FK. Use the full declared key, not a display name. Use: Audit audit id display/filter/reference; no polymorphic FK enforcement is implied.00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000101
ACTOR_USER_IDApplication actor identifier retained by the audit writer; no declared user FK. Use: Audit actor user id display/filter/reference; no polymorphic FK enforcement is implied.00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000101
ACTOR_EMAILEmail identifier supplied by the application audit writer for the actor. Use: Audit actor email display/filter/reference; no polymorphic FK enforcement is implied.example.editor@nsegg.ca
ACTIONAudit operation name supplied by application authorization/audit code. Use: Audit action display/filter/reference; no polymorphic FK enforcement is implied.CREATE
ENTITY_TYPEName of the entity/operation category recorded with the audit event. Use: Audit entity type display/filter/reference; no polymorphic FK enforcement is implied.ACCOUNT
ENTITY_IDPolymorphic audit reference to the affected record/import/batch; VARCHAR(1000), not a universal FK. Use: Audit entity id display/filter/reference; no polymorphic FK enforcement is implied.00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000101
DETAILSOptional audit JSON details supplied by the writer; no full before/after business-row history is guaranteed. Use: Audit details display/filter/reference; no polymorphic FK enforcement is implied.{"example": true}
OCCURRED_ATAudit event timestamp, stored with timezone/offset. Use: Audit occurred at display/filter/reference; no polymorphic FK enforcement is implied.2026-01-15T12:00:00+00:00

CORE.ACCOUNT

ACCOUNT stores the organization identity used by producers, graders and other business participants. Role flags allow one organization to perform several functions without creating a separate identity for each role. One business organization/account identity.

FieldDefinition and useIllustrative value
ACCOUNT_IDSource Dataverse GUID retained without name-based replacement for one business organization/account identity. Use the full declared key, not a display name. Use: Account detail/list or its recorded account id attribute; only explicit FK columns support identity joins.00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000101

ADDRESS_LINE1First stored organization/contact address line; source text is retained rather than verified against a postal service. Use: Account detail/list or its recorded address line1 attribute; only explicit FK columns support identity joins.1 Example Road

LONGITUDEOptional geographic longitude in degrees, parsed as a finite number within -180 to 180; zero is a valid coordinate. Use: Account detail/list or its recorded longitude attribute; only explicit FK columns support identity joins.-63.50

CORE.CONTACT

CONTACT preserves individual contact records, including name components, communication details and source contact metadata. Its optional Account link associates a person with an organization when that reference is available. One contact identity, optionally linked to an Account.

A. Storage metadata

FieldSQL typeNullableDefault

PROVINCETEXT(100)YESNo default declared
POSTAL_CODETEXT(50)YESNo default declared

B. Definitions, use and examples

FieldDefinition and useIllustrative value
CONTACT_IDSource Dataverse GUID retained without name-based replacement for one contact identity, optionally linked to an Account. Use the full declared key, not a display name. Use: Contact contact id in its table-specific list/detail/history; aggregate only at the documented grain.00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000101
ACCOUNT_IDAccount associated with this contact record; references CORE.ACCOUNT.ACCOUNT_ID. This role must not be replaced by another similarly named reference. Use: Contact account id in its table-specific list/detail/history; aggregate only at the documented grain.00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000101
FULL_NAMEStored contact display name; CONTACT is a list, separate from Account primary contact fields. Use: Contact full name in its table-specific list/detail/history; aggregate only at the documented grain.Example Contact

POSTAL_CODERecorded postal/ZIP text, preserving letters and formatting; numeric conversion would lose meaning. Use: Contact postal code in its table-specific list/detail/history; aggregate only at the documented grain.A1A 1A1
COUNTRY_REGIONRecorded country/region text; no authoritative country-code catalogue is enforced. Use: Contact country region in its table-specific list/detail/history; aggregate only at the documented grain.Canada
EIMS_CONTACT_NUMBERLegacy EIMS contact display/reference number preserved independently of CONTACT_ID. Use: Contact eims contact number in its table-specific list/detail/history; aggregate only at the documented grain.EX-EIMS-CONTACT-NUMBER
LANGUAGE_PREFERENCESource language preference text/code; permitted languages and code meanings require confirmation. Use: Contact language preference in its table-specific list/detail/history; aggregate only at the documented grain.EX-LANGUAGE-PREFERENCE
PRIME_CONTACTSource contact-primary flag; there is no unique filtered constraint enforcing one primary Contact per Account. Use: Contact prime contact in its table-specific list/detail/history; aggregate only at the documented grain.false
STATUSStored state/category in this table; an import transaction status, a user state and a historical Dataverse display status have different scopes. Use: Contact status in its table-specific list/detail/history; aggregate only at the documented grain.Active
CREATED_ATRecorded creation timestamp. Repository writes explicitly use UTC; historical NTZ provenance may be unverified. Use: Record provenance / modification display; UPDATED_AT is the optimistic-edit token where that writer uses it.2026-01-15 12:00:00
UPDATED_ATRecorded last modification timestamp. Historical naive values are not automatically interpreted as UTC. Use: Record provenance / modification display; UPDATED_AT is the optimistic-edit token where that writer uses it.2026-01-15 12:00:00

CORE.DIM_EFC_DATE

B. Definitions, use and examples

FieldDefinition and useIllustrative value
DAYTyped distinct calendar day key. Source rows with this same day map to one target while their separate lineages and ElementCodes remain. Use: Calendar lookup of day for DAY; not an unconfirmed Production period JOIN.2026-01-15
EFC_YEARSource EFC year assignment for DAY, not inferred from production reporting year. Use: Calendar lookup of efc year for DAY; not an unconfirmed Production period JOIN.2026

EFC_WEEKSource EFC week text/code representation; distinct from the numeric week component. Use: Calendar lookup of efc week for DAY; not an unconfirmed Production period JOIN.EX-WEEK-03
EFC_WEEK_LABELAlternative source EFCWeek1 label, not a calculated production week. Use: Calendar lookup of efc week label for DAY; not an unconfirmed Production period JOIN.Example EFC week 3
EFC_LONG_NAMESource long calendar label; preserves display evidence rather than defining a formula. Use: Calendar lookup of efc long name for DAY; not an unconfirmed Production period JOIN.Example EFC calendar label
EFC_PERIODSource EFC period assignment for DAY; a production-to-DAY rule is still required. Use: Calendar lookup of efc period for DAY; not an unconfirmed Production period JOIN.1
EFC_PERIOD_ALIASSource period alias text, not a separate period-key relation. Use: Calendar lookup of efc period alias for DAY; not an unconfirmed Production period JOIN.EX-PERIOD-01
EFC_QUARTERSource EFC quarter code; not automatically the ordinary calendar quarter. Use: Calendar lookup of efc quarter for DAY; not an unconfirmed Production period JOIN.1
ELEMENT_CODERepresentative source ElementCode for this DAY; additional codes are retained in ELEMENT_CODES and RAW. Use: Calendar lookup of element code for DAY; not an unconfirmed Production period JOIN.EX-DATE-01

PREV_YEARSource previous-EFC-year attribute; do not silently substitute DAY.year-1. Use: Calendar lookup of prev year for DAY; not an unconfirmed Production period JOIN.2025
PREV_WEEKSource previous-EFC-week attribute; week transition rules remain a supplied calendar interpretation. Use: Calendar lookup of prev week for DAY; not an unconfirmed Production period JOIN.2

DATE_VALUESource yyyymmdd parsed to a date; DAY remains the deduplication key. Use: Calendar lookup of date value for DAY; not an unconfirmed Production period JOIN.2026-01-15
MONTH_NUMBERSource calendar month numeric attribute; not an EFC period. Use: Calendar lookup of month number for DAY; not an unconfirmed Production period JOIN.1

DAYS_IN_MONTHSource number of days in the calendar month; not Production coverage. Use: Calendar lookup of days in month for DAY; not an unconfirmed Production period JOIN.31

YEAR_MONTHSource year/month display code; not a declared dimension key. Use: Calendar lookup of year month for DAY; not an unconfirmed Production period JOIN.2026-01
QUARTER_NUMBERSource ordinary calendar-quarter attribute, distinct from EFC_QUARTER. Use: Calendar lookup of quarter number for DAY; not an unconfirmed Production period JOIN.1
DAYS_IN_QUARTERSource number of days in the calendar quarter; no production quantity is derived. Use: Calendar lookup of days in quarter for DAY; not an unconfirmed Production period JOIN.90

DAY_STRINGSource textual day representation preserved beside the typed DAY key. Use: Calendar lookup of day string for DAY; not an unconfirmed Production period JOIN.2026-01-15

CORE.FACILITY

FACILITY identifies a building or housing facility owned by one recorded Account, with an optional farm-site association. Lifecycle dates and housing attributes describe that building rather than a particular Flock. One Account-owned facility/building identity; optional farm location.

CORE.FARM_LOCATION

FARM_LOCATION describes an Account-owned farm site or address identity. It stores an address and communication information separately from individual buildings at the site. One Account-owned farm-location identity.

CORE.FLOCK

FLOCK identifies one recorded chicken flock and preserves registration, lifecycle dates, bird measures and original references. A Flock has one optional Facility reference in the deployed model. One Flock identity with one optional Facility reference; multiple Flocks may share a Facility.

CORE.FLOCK_TRANSACTION

FLOCK_TRANSACTION records a dated activity or quantity event referencing a Flock. Each transaction has its own identity rather than replacing the registered Flock measures. One recorded activity/transaction referring to a Flock.

REPORTING_YEARReporting-year component: historical Production derives it from SOURCE_WEEK_CODE; import headers may summarize a single year. REPORTING_WEEKReporting-week component used with REPORTING_YEAR. Historical source-code parsing verifies weeks 1..53; this does not establish EFC period boundaries. Use: Production observation reporting week display/filter/source detail; no unapproved derived balance.15

CORE.QUOTA_TRANSACTION

QUOTA_TRANSACTION stores one dated quota activity with a primary registration and owner Account, plus optional related Account, registration and transaction roles. One quota transaction identity; related quota and related transaction are separate roles.

RAW.MIGRATION_RECONCILIATION

MIGRATION_RECONCILIATION records a batch/entity summary of source, ready, rejected and inserted rows. One batch/entity summary, not a target record.

A. Storage metadata

FieldSQL typeNullableDefault
MIGRATION_RECONCILIATION_IDTEXT(36)NONo default declared
MIGRATION_BATCH_IDTEXT(64)NONo default declared
SOURCE_ENTITYTEXT(100)NONo default declared
SOURCE_ROWSNUMBER(18,0)NONo default declared
READY_ROWSNUMBER(18,0)NONo default declared
REJECTED_ROWSNUMBER(18,0)NONo default declared
INSERTED_ROWSNUMBER(18,0)NO0
CREATED_ATTIMESTAMP_NTZ(9)NOCAST(CONVERT_TIMEZONE('UTC', CAST(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() AS TIMESTAMP_TZ(9))) AS TIMESTAMP_NTZ(9))
UPDATED_ATTIMESTAMP_NTZ(9)NOCAST(CONVERT_TIMEZONE('UTC', CAST(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() AS TIMESTAMP_TZ(9))) AS TIMESTAMP_NTZ(9))

REPORTING.VW_ACCOUNT_FACILITY

Actual deployed view definition; query joins/filters determine retention.

create or replace view VW_ACCOUNT_FACILITY(
	ACCOUNT_ID,
	REGISTRATION_NUMBER,
	ORGANIZATION_NAME,
	CITY,
	PROVINCE,
	CONTACT_NAME,
	CONTACT_PHONE,
	CONTACT_EMAIL,
	LICENCE_NUMBER,
	PRODUCER_ROLE,
	BREEDER_ROLE,
	HATCHERY_ROLE,
	GRADER_ROLE,
	ACCOUNT_STATUS,
	FACILITY_ID,
	FACILITY_NAME,
	FACILITY_TYPE,
	FACILITY_STATUS,
	ACTIVATION_DATE,
	CLOSURE_DATE
) as
SELECT
    a.ACCOUNT_ID,
    a.REGISTRATION_NUMBER,
    a.ORGANIZATION_NAME,
    a.CITY,
    a.PROVINCE,
    a.CONTACT_NAME,
    a.CONTACT_PHONE,
    a.CONTACT_EMAIL,
    a.LICENCE_NUMBER,
    a.PRODUCER_ROLE,
    a.BREEDER_ROLE,
    a.HATCHERY_ROLE,
    a.GRADER_ROLE,
    a.STATUS          AS ACCOUNT_STATUS,
    f.FACILITY_ID,
    f.FACILITY_NAME,
    f.FACILITY_TYPE,
    f.STATUS          AS FACILITY_STATUS,
    f.ACTIVATION_DATE,
    f.CLOSURE_DATE
FROM EFNS_DEV.CORE.ACCOUNT a
LEFT JOIN EFNS_DEV.CORE.FACILITY f ON a.ACCOUNT_ID = f.ACCOUNT_ID;

CITYTEXT(100)YESNo default declared
PROVINCETEXT(50)YESNo default declared
CONTACT_NAMETEXT(255)YESNo default declared
CONTACT_PHONETEXT(50)YESNo default declared
CONTACT_EMAILTEXT(255)YESNo default declared
LICENCE_NUMBERTEXT(64)YESNo default declared
PRODUCER_ROLEBOOLEANYESNo default declared
BREEDER_ROLEBOOLEANYESNo default declared

REPORTING.VW_FLOCK_DETAIL

Actual deployed view definition; query joins/filters determine retention.

create or replace view VW_FLOCK_DETAIL(
	FLOCK_ID,
	FLOCK_NUMBER,
	PERMIT_NUMBER,
	PERMIT_DATE,
	BIRD_COUNT,
	PLACEMENT_DATE,
	HATCH_DATE,
	EGG_COLOUR,
	EST_DISPOSAL,
	DISPOSAL_DATE,
	DISPOSAL_METHOD,
	FLOCK_STATUS,
	ACCOUNT_ID,
	REGISTRATION_NUMBER,
	ORGANIZATION_NAME,
	FACILITY_ID,
	FACILITY_NAME,
	FACILITY_TYPE,
	FLOCK_ACCOUNT_ID,
	FLOCK_ACCOUNT_NAME,
	FACILITY_ACCOUNT_ID,
	FACILITY_ACCOUNT_NAME
) as
SELECT
    fl.FLOCK_ID,
    fl.FLOCK_NUMBER,
    fl.PERMIT_NUMBER,
    fl.PERMIT_DATE,
    fl.BIRD_COUNT,
    fl.PLACEMENT_DATE,
    fl.HATCH_DATE,
    fl.EGG_COLOUR,
    fl.EST_DISPOSAL,
    fl.DISPOSAL_DATE,
    fl.DISPOSAL_METHOD,
    fl.STATUS                AS FLOCK_STATUS,
    fl.ACCOUNT_ID,
    a.REGISTRATION_NUMBER,
    a.ORGANIZATION_NAME,
    fl.FACILITY_ID,
    f.FACILITY_NAME,
    f.FACILITY_TYPE,
    fl.ACCOUNT_ID            AS FLOCK_ACCOUNT_ID,
    a.ORGANIZATION_NAME      AS FLOCK_ACCOUNT_NAME,
    f.ACCOUNT_ID             AS FACILITY_ACCOUNT_ID,
    fa.ORGANIZATION_NAME     AS FACILITY_ACCOUNT_NAME
FROM EFNS_DEV.CORE.FLOCK fl
LEFT JOIN EFNS_DEV.CORE.ACCOUNT a ON fl.ACCOUNT_ID = a.ACCOUNT_ID
LEFT JOIN EFNS_DEV.CORE.FACILITY f ON fl.FACILITY_ID = f.FACILITY_ID
LEFT JOIN EFNS_DEV.CORE.ACCOUNT fa ON f.ACCOUNT_ID = fa.ACCOUNT_ID;

B. Definitions, use and examples

FieldDefinition and useIllustrative value
FLOCK_IDDeployed projection `fl.FLOCK_ID` from CORE.FLOCK.FLOCK_ID: Source Dataverse GUID retained without name-based replacement for one Flock identity with one optional Facility reference; multiple Flocks may share a Facility. Use the full declared key, not a display name. Use: Projected FLOCK_ID used in VW_FLOCK_DETAIL at its stated result grain; no independent write.00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000101
FLOCK_NUMBERDeployed projection `fl.FLOCK_NUMBER` from CORE.FLOCK.FLOCK_NUMBER: Flock display/business number; historical fallback is efc_flocknumber, new_name, then source GUID. Use: Projected FLOCK_NUMBER used in VW_FLOCK_DETAIL at its stated result grain; no independent write.EX-FLOCK-01
PERMIT_NUMBERDeployed projection `fl.PERMIT_NUMBER` from CORE.FLOCK.PERMIT_NUMBER: Recorded permit text used in Flock/test display and operational matching checks; not the primary GUID. Use: Projected PERMIT_NUMBER used in VW_FLOCK_DETAIL at its stated result grain; no independent write.EX-PERMIT-01
PERMIT_DATEDeployed projection `fl.PERMIT_DATE` from CORE.FLOCK.PERMIT_DATE: Recorded permit issuance/date attribute if known; empty values remain null rather than becoming today. Use: Projected PERMIT_DATE used in VW_FLOCK_DETAIL at its stated result grain; no independent write.2026-01-15
BIRD_COUNTDeployed projection `fl.BIRD_COUNT` from CORE.FLOCK.BIRD_COUNT: Recorded registered Flock bird count, not a verified current-inventory calculation. Use: Projected BIRD_COUNT used in VW_FLOCK_DETAIL at its stated result grain; no independent write.1000

REPORTING.VW_PRODUCTION_SIZE_DETAIL

Actual deployed view definition; query joins/filters determine retention.

create or replace view VW_PRODUCTION_SIZE_DETAIL(
	PRODUCTION_ID,
	GRADER_NUMBER,
	BARN_IDENTITY,
	EGG_COLOUR,
	SIZE_BREAKDOWN_ID,
	SIZE_BAND,
	QUANTITY,
	REPORTING_YEAR,
	REPORTING_WEEK
) as
SELECT
    pr.PRODUCTION_ID,
    pr.GRADER_NUMBER,
    pr.BARN_IDENTITY,
    pr.EGG_COLOUR,
    psb.SIZE_BREAKDOWN_ID,
    psb.SIZE_BAND,
    psb.QUANTITY,
    pr.REPORTING_YEAR,
    pr.REPORTING_WEEK
FROM EFNS_DEV.CORE.PRODUCTION_RECORD pr
LEFT JOIN EFNS_DEV.CORE.PRODUCTION_SIZE_BREAKDOWN psb ON pr.PRODUCTION_ID = psb.PRODUCTION_ID;

REPORTING.VW_PRODUCTION_SUMMARY

Actual deployed view definition; query joins/filters determine retention.

create or replace view VW_PRODUCTION_SUMMARY(
	PRODUCTION_ID,
	FILENAME,
	SOURCE,
	REPORTING_YEAR,
	REPORTING_WEEK,
	UPLOAD_TIMESTAMP,
	GRADER_NUMBER,
	PRODUCER_NUMBER,
	PRODUCER_ACCOUNT_ID,
	GRADER_ACCOUNT_ID,
	FACILITY_ID,
	FLOCK_ID,
	BARN_IDENTITY,
	FLOCK_AGE,
	EGG_COLOUR,
	NET_WEIGHT,
	NET_BOXES,
	NET_PER_BOX,
	TOTAL_RECEIVED,
	REJECTED,
	LOSS,
	LEGACY_REJECT_LOSS_TOTAL,
	TOTAL_ACCEPTED,
	SOURCE_TYPE,
	MATCH_STATUS,
	SOURCE_ROW_NUMBER,
	CREATED_AT,
	UPDATED_AT,
	TOTAL
) as
SELECT
    pr.PRODUCTION_ID,
    ib.FILENAME,
    ib.SOURCE,
    pr.REPORTING_YEAR,
    pr.REPORTING_WEEK,
    ib.UPLOAD_TIMESTAMP,
    pr.GRADER_NUMBER,
    pr.PRODUCER_NUMBER,
    pr.PRODUCER_ACCOUNT_ID,
    pr.GRADER_ACCOUNT_ID,
    pr.FACILITY_ID,
    pr.FLOCK_ID,
    pr.BARN_IDENTITY,
    pr.FLOCK_AGE,
    pr.EGG_COLOUR,
    pr.NET_WEIGHT,
    pr.NET_BOXES,
    pr.NET_PER_BOX,
    pr.TOTAL_RECEIVED,
    pr.REJECTED,
    pr.LOSS,
    pr.LEGACY_REJECT_LOSS_TOTAL,
    pr.TOTAL_ACCEPTED,
    pr.SOURCE_TYPE,
    pr.MATCH_STATUS,
    pr.SOURCE_ROW_NUMBER,
    pr.CREATED_AT,
    pr.UPDATED_AT,
    pr.TOTAL
FROM EFNS_DEV.CORE.PRODUCTION_RECORD pr
LEFT JOIN EFNS_DEV.RAW.IMPORT_BATCH ib ON pr.IMPORT_ID = ib.IMPORT_ID;

SECURITY.APP_USER

APP_USER stores an application user identity, email/display label, application role, active state and login/maintenance timestamps. One application user/security identity.

A. Storage metadata

FieldSQL typeNullableDefault
USER_IDTEXT(36)NONo default declared
EMAILTEXT(320)NONo default declared
DISPLAY_NAMETEXT(255)NONo default declared
ROLETEXT(50)NONo default declared
ACTIVEBOOLEANNOTRUE
LAST_LOGIN_ATTIMESTAMP_TZ(9)YESNo default declared
CREATED_ATTIMESTAMP_TZ(9)NOCONVERT_TIMEZONE('UTC', CAST(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() AS TIMESTAMP_TZ(9)))
UPDATED_ATTIMESTAMP_TZ(9)NOCONVERT_TIMEZONE('UTC', CAST(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() AS TIMESTAMP_TZ(9)))

B. Definitions, use and examples

FieldDefinition and useIllustrative value
USER_IDApplication-user registry identity; not an EFNS business Account or Snowflake database role. Use: Application identity/permission registry user id, separate from business Account ownership.00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000101
EMAILApplication login/registry email used for identity lookup and user management; declared UNIQUE, but not engine-enforced on this standard table. Use: Application identity/permission registry email, separate from business Account ownership.example.editor@nsegg.ca
DISPLAY_NAMEApplication-user display text, distinct from login email and business organization names. Use: Application identity/permission registry display name, separate from business Account ownership.Example editor
ROLEApplication permission role (Admin, Data Editor, Reporting Viewer, Developer), separate from Snowflake grant roles. Use: Application identity/permission registry role, separate from business Account ownership.Data Editor
ACTIVEApplication-user enabled state; exact retention policy after deactivation is still a business/security decision. Use: Application identity/permission registry active, separate from business Account ownership.false
LAST_LOGIN_ATOptional application-user registry time selected for display. No current Snowflake login-time update method was found; it is not proof of capturing each login or database connection. Use: Application identity/permission registry last login at, separate from business Account ownership.2026-01-15T12:00:00+00:00
CREATED_ATRecorded creation timestamp. Repository writes explicitly use UTC; historical NTZ provenance may be unverified. Use: Record provenance / modification display; UPDATED_AT is the optimistic-edit token where that writer uses it.2026-01-15T12:00:00+00:00
UPDATED_ATRecorded last modification timestamp. Historical naive values are not automatically interpreted as UTC. Use: Record provenance / modification display; UPDATED_AT is the optimistic-edit token where that writer uses it.2026-01-15T12:00:00+00:00
